def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """4200-calculate_premiums."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate READ insurance_master NEXT
        # Assume insurance_master_record is populated here
        insurance_master_record = INSURANCE_MASTER  # Assign to avoid unbound error
        if insurance_master_record:  # NOT AT END condition
            determine_base_premium_C2()
            apply_risk_factor_C2()
            calculate_final_premium_C2()
        else:  # AT END condition
            WS_EOF = True

def determine_base_premium_C2() -> None:
    """4210-determine_base_premium."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_life:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
    elif INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_health:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_auto:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_home:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
    elif INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_umbrella:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

def apply_risk_factor_C2() -> None:
    """4220-apply_risk_factor."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if INSURANCE_MASTER.ins_claims_count > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal('1.25')

    """4230-calculate_final_premium."""

    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_position_value_C2() -> None:
    """5210-calculate_position_value."""
    INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value = INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_quantity * INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_current_price

def calculate_gain_loss_C2() -> None:
    """5220-calculate_gain_loss."""
    INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_gain_loss = INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value - (INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_quantity * INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_purchase_price)

def update_totals_C2() -> None:
    """5230-update_totals."""
    global WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_market_value

    WS_VALID = True
    if ACCT_ID == "":
        WS_INVALID = True

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """8500-calculate_tax."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
    else:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def calculate_risk_score_C2() -> None:
    """7410-calculate_risk_score."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = Decimal('0')
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_credit_score < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans > CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 20

def update_customer_profile_C2() -> None:
    """7420-update_customer_profile."""
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 50:
        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_risk_rating = 'H'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_risk_rating = 'M'
    else:
        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_risk_rating = 'L'

def dti_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """7821-dti_calculation."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_NOT_APPROVED
    WS_CALC_RESULT = LOAN_MASTER.loan_payment_amount / (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance / 12)
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal('0.43'):
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def ltv_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """7822-ltv_calculation."""
    global LOAN_LTV_RATIO, WS_CALC_FEE
    LOAN_MASTER.loan_ltv_ratio = LOAN_MASTER.loan_current_balance / LOAN_MASTER.loan_collateral_value
    if LOAN_MASTER.loan_ltv_ratio > Decimal('0.80'):
        WS_CALC_FEE += WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT

def credit_analysis_C2() -> None:
    """7823-credit_analysis."""
    global WS_NOT_APPROVED
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_credit_score < 620:
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def calculate_returns_C2() -> None:
    """7911-calculate_returns."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    if INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_purchase_price > 0:
        WS_CALC_RESULT = (INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_current_price - INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_purchase_price) / INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_purchase_price * 100

def assess_risk_C2() -> None:
    """7912-assess_risk."""
    global WS_TEMP_FLAG
    if INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_stocks:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'H'
    elif INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_bonds:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'L'
    elif INVESTMENT_MASTER.inv_mutual_fund:

        pass

@dataclass
class CustomerMaster:
    """Data class for customer_master"""
    cust_id: str = ""
    cust_name: str = ""
    cust_last_name: str = ""
    cust_state: str = ""
    cust_credit_score: int = 0
    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    cust_last_activity: int = 0 # Assuming YYYYMMDD format

ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ACCT_MIN_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
LOAN_DELINQUENT: bool = False

CUSTOMER_MASTER: Optional[CustomerMaster] = None

WS_ANNUAL_FEE_CARD: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_WIRE_FEE_DOMESTIC: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_WIRE_FEE_INTL: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_SAVINGS_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_PERSONAL_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TEMP_CODE: str = ""
WS_EOF: bool = False
WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
WS_NOT_APPROVED: bool = False
WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
WS_CURRENT_DATE: int = 0

    """9310-customer_segmentation."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, CUSTOMER_MASTER, CUSTOMER_MASTER

    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading from customer_master
        # In a real implementation, this would involve file I/O or database access
        # For this example, let's assume CUSTOMER_MASTER is populated from a list'
        
        if CUSTOMER_MASTER:
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()
        else:
            WS_EOF = True
        
        WS_EOF = True

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """9311-calculate_clv."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_SAVINGS_RATE, WS_PERSONAL_RATE

    if CUSTOMER_MASTER is not None:
        WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_balance * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                         (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_loans * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                         (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_total_investments * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """9312-assign_segment."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def extract_data_A110_C3() -> None:
    """A110-extract_data."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, WS_PROCESS_COUNT, CUSTOMER_MASTER

    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Simulate reading from customer_master
        # In a real implementation, this would involve file I/O or database access
        # For this example, let's assume CUSTOMER_MASTER is populated from a list'

        if CUSTOMER_MASTER:
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
        else:
            WS_EOF = True

        WS_EOF = True

def transform_data_A120_C3() -> None:
    """A120-transform_data."""
    cleanse_data_A121_C3()
    standardize_data_A122_C3()
    enrich_data_A123_C3()

def cleanse_data_A121_C3() -> None:
    """A121-cleanse_data."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER and not CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_name.strip():
        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_last_name = "UNKNOWN"

def standardize_data_A122_C3() -> None:
    """A122-standardize_data."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER:
        CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_state = CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_state.upper()

def completeness_check_A210_C3() -> None:
    """A210-completeness_check."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_ERROR_COUNT
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER and not CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_id.strip():
        WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

def accuracy_check_A220_C3() -> None:
    """A220-accuracy_check."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_ERROR_COUNT
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER and (CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_credit_score < 300 or CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_credit_score > 850):
        WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

def consistency_check_A230_C3() -> None:
    """A230-consistency_check."""
    continue_C3()

def timeliness_check_A240_C3() -> None:
    """A240-timeliness_check."""
    global CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_CURRENT_DATE, WS_ERROR_COUNT
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER and CUSTOMER_MASTER.cust_last_activity < WS_CURRENT_DATE - 365:
        WS_ERROR_COUNT += 1

@dataclass
class TransactionLog:
    """Represents a transaction log record."""
    tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class LoanRecord:
    """Represents a loan record."""
    loan_current_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    loan_paid_off: bool = False

cust_status: str = ""
ws_temp_code: str = ""
cust_ssn: str = ""
ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_eof: bool = False
tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_error_count: int = 0
cust_credit_score: int = 0
cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
cust_risk_rating: str = ""
ws_current_timestamp: str = ""
ws_temp_string: str = ""
ws_valid: bool = False
ws_atm_fee_foreign: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
ws_cust_count: int = 0
ws_formatted_count: str = ""
transaction_log: Optional[TransactionLog] = None
loan_record: Optional[LoanRecord] = None

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """C100-transaction_monitoring"""
    DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        READ transaction_log NEXT
            AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM C110-rule_based_detection
                PERFORM C120-behavior_analysis
                PERFORM C130-network_analysis
        
    
    """"""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof, transaction_log"""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    ws_eof = False
    while not ws_eof:
        # Simulate reading a transaction log
        if transaction_log:
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
            c130_network_analysis_C4()
            transaction_log = None  # Simulate reading only one record
        else:
            ws_eof = True

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global tran_amount
    if tran_amount >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4()
    if 5000 <= tran_amount < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4()

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global cust_risk_rating, cust_credit_score
    if cust_credit_score > 750:
        cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif cust_credit_score > 650:
        cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif cust_credit_score > 550:
        cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_calc_result, cust_credit_score, cust_total_balance, cust_total_loans
    ws_calc_result = Decimal((cust_credit_score * 10) + 0  # TODO
                            (cust_total_balance / 1000) - 0  # TODO
                            (cust_total_loans / 2000))

def main_program():
    """Top level method"""
    set_ws_not_eof_to_true()
    while not is_ws_eof():
        read_customer_master_next()

def set_ws_not_eof_to_true():
    # COBOL reference preserved
    global WS_NOT_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True

    global WS_CURRENT_DATE, CUST_LAST_ACTIVITY

    CUST_LAST_ACTIVITY  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURRENT_DATE

def _0000_main_control_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    _1000_initialization_C5()
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        _2000_process_transactions_C5()
    _9000_finalization_C5()
    # STOP RUN equivalent: program ends

def _1000_initialization_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS, WS_COUNTERS, WS_TOTALS, WS_CURRENT_DATETIME, RPT_YEAR, RPT_MONTH, RPT_DAY

    WS_WORK_AREAS = WsWorkAreas()
    WS_COUNTERS = WsCounters()
    WS_TOTALS = WsTotals()
    WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = str(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")) #YYYYMMDD
    RPT_YEAR = str(datetime.now().year)
    RPT_MONTH = str(datetime.now().month)
    RPT_DAY = str(datetime.now().day)
    _1100_open_files_C5()
    _1200_read_parameters_C5()
    _1300_initialize_tables_C5()
    _1400_load_reference_data_C5()

def _1100_open_files_C5() -> None:
    """1100-open_files
    OPEN INPUT  customer_file
    OPEN INPUT  account_file
    OPEN INPUT  transaction_file
    OPEN OUTPUT report_file
    OPEN OUTPUT error_file
    OPEN I-O    master_file
    IF ws_file_status NOT = '00'
        MOVE 'FILE OPEN ERROR' TO ws_error_msg
        PERFORM 9500-abort_process
    
    global CUSTOMER_FILE, ACCOUNT_FILE, TRANSACTION_FILE, REPORT_FILE, ERROR_FILE, MASTER_FILE, WS_FILE_STATUS, WS_ERROR_MSG
    """
    # Replace with actual file opening logic"""
    # Simulate file opening"""
    CUSTOMER_FILE = "customer.txt"
    ACCOUNT_FILE = "account.txt"
    TRANSACTION_FILE = "transaction.txt"
    REPORT_FILE = "report.txt"
    ERROR_FILE = "error.txt"
    MASTER_FILE = "master.txt"

    WS_FILE_STATUS = "00"  # Simulate successful file opening
    
    if WS_FILE_STATUS != '00':
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        _9500_abort_process_C5()

def _1200_read_parameters_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_PARAM_DATE, WS_PARAM_TIME, WS_JOB_ID, WS_ENV_TYPE, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    
    WS_PARAM_DATE = str(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")) #YYYYMMDD
    WS_PARAM_TIME = str(datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")) #HHMMSS
    WS_JOB_ID = 'batch_001'
    WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
    WS_PROCESS_DATE = int(WS_PARAM_DATE) #integer_of_date

def _1300_initialize_tables_C5() -> None:
    """1300-initialize_tables
    PERFORM VARYING ws_tbl_idx FROM 1 BY 1
        UNTIL ws_tbl_idx > 100
        INITIALIZE rate_table_entry(ws_tbl_idx)
        MOVE ZEROES TO rt_rate(ws_tbl_idx)
        MOVE SPACES TO rt_code(ws_tbl_idx)
    
    PERFORM VARYING ws_tbl_idx FROM 1 BY 1
        UNTIL ws_tbl_idx > 50
        INITIALIZE branch_table_entry(ws_tbl_idx)
    
    global WS_TBL_IDX, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY, BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY

    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 101):"""
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = RateTableEntry()"""
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1].rt_rate = Decimal('0.00')"""
        RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1].rt_code = ""

    for WS_TBL_IDX in range(1, 51):
        BRANCH_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1] = BranchTableEntry()

def _1400_load_reference_data_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TBL_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_REF_RECORD, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY, REFERENCE_FILE

    WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = "N"
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX <= 100:
        # Simulate reading from reference_file
        # Replace this with actual file reading logic
        ref_data = None #Replace with data read from reference_file
        if ref_data is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_REF_RECORD = WsRefRecord() #populate data

            RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1].rt_code = WS_REF_RECORD.ws_ref_code
            RATE_TABLE_ENTRY[WS_TBL_IDX - 1].rt_rate = WS_REF_RECORD.ws_ref_rate
            WS_TBL_IDX += 1

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def _2000_process_transactions_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""2100-validate_transaction
    MOVE 'Y' TO ws_valid_flag
    IF txn_account_id = SPACES OR low_values
        MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
        MOVE 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID' TO ws_error_msg
        EXIT PARAGRAPH
    
    IF txn_amount IS NOT NUMERIC
        MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
        MOVE 'INVALID AMOUNT' TO ws_error_msg
        EXIT PARAGRAPH
    
    IF txn_type NOT = 'D' AND txn_type NOT = 'W'
        AND txn_type NOT = 'T' AND txn_type NOT = 'I'
        MOVE 'N' TO ws_valid_flag
        MOVE 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE' TO ws_error_msg
    
    PERFORM 2150-validate_account_exists
    PERFORM 2160-validate_business_rules."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID, WS_ERROR_MSG, TXN_AMOUNT, TXN_TYPE
    WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    
    if TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == "" or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID == "":
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    
    try:
        float(TXN_AMOUNT)  # Check if TXN_AMOUNT is numeric
    except ValueError:
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    
    if TXN_TYPE not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):
        WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'
    
    _2150_validate_account_exists_C5()
    _2160_validate_business_rules_C5()

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """10335-calculate_pmi"""
    """
    match True:
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
        case ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
        case _:
            ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount"""

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:"""
    """10340-evaluate_history"""
    """
    ws_factor_1 = ''
    ws_factor_2 = ''
    ws_factor_3 = ''
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3"""

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[Decimal, str]:"""
    """10350-calculate_final_risk"""
    """
    ws_risk_score_decimal = Decimal(str(ws_risk_score)) / 4
    ws_risk_category = ''
    match True:
        case ws_risk_score_decimal >= 80:
            ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
        case ws_risk_score_decimal >= 60:
            ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
        case ws_risk_score_decimal >= 40:
            ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
        case _:
            ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score_decimal, ws_risk_category"""
""""""
def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:"""
    """10400-determine_approval"""
    """
    ws_approval_status = ''
    ws_conditions = ''
    ws_approved_amount = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal('0.00')
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount, ws_credit_tier, ws_base_rate, ws_risk_category)
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_amount"""
""""""
def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_credit_tier: str, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_risk_category: str) -> Decimal:"""
    """10450-calculate_approved_terms"""
    """
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    match ws_credit_tier:
        case 'A':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
        case 'B':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
        case 'C':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
        case 'D':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
        case _:
            ws_approved_rate = Decimal('0.00')  # Handle other cases if needed
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')
    return ws_approved_rate"""
""""""
def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:"""
    """10500-generate_loan_terms"""
    """
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt"""
""""""
def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal) -> list[AmortizationEntry]:"""
    """10600-create_amortization"""
    """
    ws_running_balance = ws_loan_amount
    ws_payment_date = datetime.now() # Current date
    ws_payment_month = ws_payment_date.month
    ws_payment_year = ws_payment_date.year
    amortization_schedule = []
    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amort_interest, amort_principal, ws_running_balance = calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance, ws_monthly_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, loan_mortgage, ws_property_tax, ws_insurance_premium, ws_pmi_amount)

        amort_payment_date = advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)
        ws_payment_month = amort_payment_date[0]
        ws_payment_year = amort_payment_date[1]

        amort_entry = AmortizationEntry()
        amort_entry.amort_interest = amort_interest
        amort_entry.amort_principal = amort_principal
        amort_entry.amort_balance = ws_running_balance
        amort_entry.amort_payment_num = ws_amort_idx
        amort_entry.amort_payment_amt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
        if loan_mortgage:
            amort_entry.amort_escrow = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
            amort_entry.amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_entry.amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
        else:
            amort_entry.amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt

        amort_entry.amort_payment_date = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 1

        amortization_schedule.append(amort_entry)

    return amortization_schedule"""
""""""
def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:"""
    """10650-calculate_payment_split"""
    """
    amort_interest = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal

    return amort_interest, amort_principal, ws_running_balance"""
""""""
def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[int, int]:"""
    """10660-advance_payment_date"""
    """
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1
    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year"""

""""""
def load_portfolio_C7() -> tuple[list[WsHoldingRec], int]:"""
    """11100-load_portfolio"""
    """
    ws_hold_idx = 1
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    ws_holding = []
    ws_holding_rec = WsHoldingRec()

    #Simulate reading from holdings_file
    holdings_data = [
    WsHoldingRec(hold_symbol='AAPL', hold_shares=100, hold_cost_per_share=Decimal('150.00'), hold_current_price=Decimal('170.00'), hold_type='STK'),
    WsHoldingRec(hold_symbol='GOOG', hold_shares=50, hold_cost_per_share=Decimal('2500.00'), hold_current_price=Decimal('2700.00'), hold_type='STK'),
    WsHoldingRec(hold_symbol='AGG', hold_shares=200, hold_cost_per_share=Decimal('110.00'), hold_current_price=Decimal('112.00'), hold_type='BND'),
    ]
    holdings_index = 0
    while ws_hold_idx <= 100 and ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        try:
           ws_holding_rec = holdings_data[holdings_index]

        except IndexError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            break
        ws_holding.append(ws_holding_rec)
        ws_hold_idx += 1
        holdings_index +=1


    ws_holdings_count = ws_hold_idx - 1
    return ws_holding, ws_holdings_count"""
""""""
def update_market_prices_C7(ws_holding: list[WsHoldingRec], ws_holdings_count: int) -> list[WsHoldingRec]:"""

        ws_quote_price = get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol)
        ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_current_price = ws_quote_price
    return ws_holding"""
""""""
def get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol: str) -> Decimal:"""
    """11250-get_quote"""
    """
    quote_request = QuoteRequest(quote_request_symbol=ws_quote_symbol)
    quote_response = get_quote_external(quote_request) # Calling external function
    if quote_response.quote_response_status == 'OK':
        ws_quote_price = quote_response.quote_last_price
    else:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal('0.00')
    return ws_quote_price

def get_quote_external(quote_request: QuoteRequest) -> QuoteResponse:
    # Simulate external call
    if quote_request.quote_request_symbol == 'AAPL':
        return QuoteResponse(quote_last_price=Decimal('175.00'), quote_response_status='OK')
    elif quote_request.quote_request_symbol == 'GOOG':
        return QuoteResponse(quote_last_price=Decimal('2750.00'), quote_response_status='OK')
    elif quote_request.quote_request_symbol == 'AGG':
        return QuoteResponse(quote_last_price=Decimal('113.00'), quote_response_status='OK')
    else:
        return QuoteResponse(quote_last_price=Decimal('0.00'), quote_response_status='ERROR')"""

def calculate_values_C7(ws_holding: list[WsHoldingRec], ws_holdings_count: int) -> tuple[list[WsHoldingRec], Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:"""
    """11300-calculate_values"""
    """
    ws_total_value = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_cost_basis = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal('0.00')
    for ws_hold_idx in range(ws_holdings_count):
        ws_holding[ws_hold_idx], ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain = calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_holding[ws_hold_idx], ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain)
    return ws_holding, ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain"""
""""""
def calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_holding_rec: WsHoldingRec, ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_cost_basis: Decimal, ws_unrealized_gain: Decimal) -> tuple[WsHoldingRec, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:"""
    """11350-calculate_holding_value"""
    """
    hold_market_value = ws_holding_rec.hold_shares * ws_holding_rec.hold_current_price
    ws_hold_cost = ws_holding_rec.hold_shares * ws_holding_rec.hold_cost_per_share
    hold_gain_loss = hold_market_value - ws_hold_cost
    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        hold_pct_change = (hold_gain_loss / ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        hold_pct_change = Decimal('0.00')

    ws_holding_rec.hold_market_value = hold_market_value
    ws_holding_rec.hold_gain_loss = hold_gain_loss
    ws_holding_rec.hold_pct_change = hold_pct_change

    ws_total_value += hold_market_value
    ws_cost_basis += ws_hold_cost
    ws_unrealized_gain += hold_gain_loss

    return ws_holding_rec, ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain"""
""""""
def rebalance_check_C7(ws_holding: list[WsHoldingRec], ws_holdings_count: int, ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> None:"""
    """11400-rebalance_check"""
    """
    ws_stocks_value, ws_bonds_value, ws_cash_value = calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_holding, ws_holdings_count)
    ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct = calculate_percentages_C7(ws_stocks_value, ws_bonds_value, ws_cash_value, ws_total_value)
    ws_rebalance_needed = compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_target_stocks_pct, ws_target_bonds_pct)
    if ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_total_value, ws_target_stocks_pct)"""
""""""
def calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_holding: list[WsHoldingRec], ws_holdings_count: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:"""
    """11410-calculate_current_allocation"""
    """
    ws_stocks_value = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_bonds_value = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_cash_value = Decimal('0.00')
    for ws_hold_idx in range(ws_holdings_count):
        match ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_type:
            case 'STK':
                ws_stocks_value += ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_market_value
            case 'BND':
                ws_bonds_value += ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_market_value
            case 'CSH':
                ws_cash_value += ws_holding[ws_hold_idx].hold_market_value
            case _:
                pass # Handle other cases if needed
    return ws_stocks_value, ws_bonds_value, ws_cash_value
"""
def calculate_percentages_C7(ws_stocks_value: Decimal, ws_bonds_value: Decimal, ws_cash_value: Decimal, ws_total_value: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:"""
""""""
    """Calculate allocation percentages"""
    ws_stocks_pct = (ws_stocks_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_bonds_pct = (ws_bonds_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_cash_pct = (ws_cash_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    return ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct

def compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_bonds_pct: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> str:

def _13220_calc_auto_premium_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13220-calc_auto_premium."""
    data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = data_storage.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.005") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    match data_storage.WS_DRIVER_AGE: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 6 <= age <= 10: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 100 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case _: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 50 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = data_storage.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data_storage.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = data_storage.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data_storage.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    data_storage.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    data_storage.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data_storage.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / 12 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13230_calc_home_premium_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13230-calc_home_premium."""
    data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = data_storage.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.003") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    match data_storage.WS_HOME_AGE: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.2") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case _: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    data_storage.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = data_storage.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / 1000 * 50 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= data_storage.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM < 200: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(200) # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    data_storage.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    data_storage.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data_storage.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / 12 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13240_calc_health_premium_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13240-calc_health_premium."""
    data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(300) # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    match data_storage.WS_INSURED_AGE: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.5") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.0") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case _: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.8") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    match data_storage.WS_PLAN_TYPE: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case 'BRONZE': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.8") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case 'SILVER': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case 'GOLD': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case 'PLATINUM': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.5") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    data_storage.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data_storage.WS_BASE_PREMIUM # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    data_storage.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data_storage.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * 12 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13300_underwriting_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13300-UNDERWRITING."""
    _13310_evaluate_risk_factors_C8(data_storage) # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    _13320_check_medical_history_C8(data_storage) # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    _13330_verify_information_C8(data_storage) # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    _13340_determine_decision_C8(data_storage) # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13310_evaluate_risk_factors_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13310-evaluate_risk_factors."""
    data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS = 0 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    if data_storage.POLICY_LIFE: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        if data_storage.WS_BMI > 30: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

        if data_storage.WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 25 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

        if data_storage.WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.POLICY_AUTO: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        if data_storage.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

        if data_storage.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13320_check_medical_history_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13320-check_medical_history."""
    if data_storage.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_CONDITION_POINTS = data_storage.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += data_storage.WS_CONDITION_POINTS # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 5 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13330_verify_information_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13330-verify_information."""
    _13335_check_fraud_indicators_C8(data_storage) # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    _13336_validate_documents_C8(data_storage) # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13335_check_fraud_indicators_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13335-check_fraud_indicators."""
    if data_storage.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y' # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

    if data_storage.WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10 # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13336_validate_documents_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13336-validate_documents."""
    if data_storage.WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y': # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING' # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
    else: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        data_storage.WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE' # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def _13340_determine_decision_C8(data_storage: DataStorage) -> None:
    """13340-determine_decision."""
    match True: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case bool(data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS > 50): # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE' # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case bool(data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS > 30): # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD' # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case bool(data_storage.WS_RISK_POINTS > 15): # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD' # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
        case _: # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED' # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt
            data_storage.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9") # COBOL line number not provided in the prompt

def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_period: str, ws_last_run_date: str) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    global WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE
    iod_last_run = int(ws_last_run_date) #mock
    match ws_period:
        case 'DAILY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = iod_last_run + 1
        case 'WEEKLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = iod_last_run + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = iod_last_run + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = iod_last_run + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = iod_last_run + 365

def data_analytics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL: 21000-data_analytics."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21100-collect_metrics."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21110-collect_transaction_metrics."""
    global WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_total_trans_amount
    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_total_trans_count
    WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_avg_trans_amount
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = TRANSACTION_FILE.pop(0)  # READ transaction_file INTO ws_trans_rec
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO ws_total_trans_count
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT += ws_trans_rec.trans_amount  # ADD trans_amount TO ws_total_trans_amount
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
    if WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:  # IF ws_total_trans_count > 0
        WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT / WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT  # COMPUTE ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL: 21120-collect_customer_metrics."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21130-collect_performance_metrics."""
    global WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL, WS_RESPONSE_COUNT, WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_response_time_total
    WS_RESPONSE_COUNT = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_response_count
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = PERF_LOG_FILE.pop(0)  # READ perf_log_file INTO ws_perf_rec
            WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL += ws_perf_rec.perf_response_time  # ADD perf_response_time TO ws_response_time_total
            WS_RESPONSE_COUNT += 1  # ADD 1 TO ws_response_count
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
    if WS_RESPONSE_COUNT > 0:  # IF ws_response_count > 0
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME = WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL / WS_RESPONSE_COUNT  # COMPUTE ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

    # WRITE daily_summary_record FROM ws_daily_summary (replace with file write logic)

def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL: 21220-weekly_aggregation."""
    global WS_DAY_OF_WEEK, WS_WEEK_NUMBER, WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD
    if WS_DAY_OF_WEEK == 7:  # IF ws_day_of_week = 7
        WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD = WsWeeklySummary()  # INITIALIZE ws_weekly_summary
        WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.weekly_week = WS_WEEK_NUMBER  # MOVE ws_week_number TO weekly_week
        sum_week_data_C10()  # PERFORM 21225-sum_week_data
        # WRITE weekly_summary_record FROM ws_weekly_summary (replace with file write logic)
        print(f"Writing weekly summary: {WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD}")

def sum_week_data_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL: 21225-sum_week_data."""
    global WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD
    WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.weekly_trans_count = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_count
    WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_amount
    for _ in range(7):  # PERFORM 7 TIMES
        WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.weekly_trans_count += DAILY_SUMMARY_RECORD.daily_trans_count # ADD daily_trans_count TO weekly_trans_count
        WEEKLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.weekly_trans_amount += DAILY_SUMMARY_RECORD.daily_trans_amount # ADD daily_trans_amount TO weekly_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL: 21230-monthly_aggregation."""
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH, WS_CURR_MONTH, WS_CURR_YEAR, MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD
    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':  # IF ws_end_of_month = 'Y'
        MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD = WsMonthlySummary()  # INITIALIZE ws_monthly_summary
        MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.monthly_month = WS_CURR_MONTH  # MOVE ws_curr_month TO monthly_month
        MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.monthly_year = WS_CURR_YEAR  # MOVE ws_curr_year TO monthly_year
        sum_month_data_C10()  # PERFORM 21235-sum_month_data
        # WRITE monthly_summary_record FROM ws_monthly_summary (replace with file write logic)
        print(f"Writing monthly summary: {MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD}")

def sum_month_data_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL: 21235-sum_month_data."""
    global MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CURR_MONTH
    MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.monthly_trans_count = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_trans_count
    MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00")  # MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_trans_amount
    MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.monthly_new_accounts = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_new_accounts
    MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.monthly_closed_accounts = 0  # MOVE ZEROES TO monthly_closed_accounts
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':  # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = DAILY_SUMMARY_FILE.pop(0)  # READ daily_summary_file INTO ws_daily_sum_rec
            if ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_month == WS_CURR_MONTH:  # IF daily_month = ws_curr_month
                MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.monthly_trans_count += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count  # ADD daily_trans_count TO monthly_trans_count
                MONTHLY_SUMMARY_RECORD.monthly_trans_amount += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount  # ADD daily_trans_amount TO monthly_trans_amount
        except IndexError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'  # MOVE 'Y' TO ws_eof_flag
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'  # MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag

def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL: 21300-calculate_kpi."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21310-calc_financial_kpi."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21320-calc_operational_kpi."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21330-calc_customer_kpi."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21400-generate_dashboard."""COBOL logic"""COBOL: 21410-create_executive_dashboard."""

    WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD.dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'  # MOVE 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD' TO dash_title
    WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD.dash_revenue = WS_TOTAL_REVENUE  # MOVE ws_total_revenue TO dash_revenue
    WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD.dash_net_income = WS_NET_INCOME  # MOVE ws_net_income TO dash_net_income
    WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD.dash_roa = WS_ROA  # MOVE ws_roa TO dash_roa
    WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD.dash_roe = WS_ROE  # MOVE ws_roe TO dash_roe
    WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD.dash_customers = WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS  # MOVE ws_active_customers TO dash_customers
    DASHBOARD_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_EXEC_DASHBOARD
    # WRITE dashboard_record FROM ws_exec_dashboard (replace with file write logic)
    print(f"Writing executive dashboard: {DASHBOARD_RECORD}")

def shipping_logic_C11(ws_process_date: str, ws_shipment_record: WsShipmentRecordC11) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    process_date = datetime.datetime.strptime(ws_process_date, "%Y%m%d").date()
    if process_date.weekday() < 5:
        ws_shipment_record.ship_method = 'EXPRESS'
        ws_shipment_record.ship_est_delivery = int(process_date.toordinal()) + 2
    else:
        ws_shipment_record.ship_method = 'STANDARD'
        ws_shipment_record.ship_est_delivery = int(process_date.toordinal()) + 7
    # Assuming WRITE shipment_record is handled elsewhere as file IO

def card_blocking_C11(ws_block_reason: str, ws_process_date: str, ws_card_record: WsCardRecordC11, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_body: str, send_notification: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_card_record.card_status = 'B'
    ws_card_record.card_block_reason = ws_block_reason
    ws_card_record.card_block_date = ws_process_date
    # Assuming REWRITE card_record is handled elsewhere as file IO
    ws_notif_type = 'card_blocked'
    ws_notif_channel = 'SMS'
    ws_notif_body = f'Your card has been blocked: {ws_block_reason}'
    send_notification()

def wire_transfer_C11(ws_wire_valid: str, ws_ofac_clear: str, validate_wire_request: callable, ofac_screening: callable, process_wire: callable, send_confirmation: callable, reject_wire: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    validate_wire_request()
    if ws_wire_valid == 'Y':
        ofac_screening()
        if ws_ofac_clear == 'Y':
            process_wire()
            send_confirmation()
        else:
            reject_wire()

def validate_wire_request_C11(ws_wire_valid: str, ws_wire_amount: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_beneficiary_account: str, ws_wire_reject: str, ws_ctr_required: str) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_wire_valid = 'Y'
    if ws_wire_amount <= 0:
        ws_wire_valid = 'N'
        ws_wire_reject = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if ws_wire_amount > ws_account_balance:
        ws_wire_valid = 'N'
        ws_wire_reject = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if ws_beneficiary_account == '':  # Assuming SPACES is an empty string
        ws_wire_valid = 'N'
        ws_wire_reject = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if ws_wire_amount > 10000:
        ws_ctr_required = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11(ws_ofac_clear: str, ws_beneficiary_name: str, ofac_search_name: str, ofac_search_bank: str, ofac_match_found: str, ofac_match_score: int, ws_wire_reject: str, ws_beneficiary_bank: str, ofac_request: OfacRequestC11, ofac_response: OfacResponseC11, ofacsrch: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_ofac_clear = 'Y'
    ofac_search_name = ws_beneficiary_name
    ofacsrch(ofac_request, ofac_response)
    if ofac_match_found == 'Y':
        if ofac_match_score >= 85:
            ws_ofac_clear = 'N'
            ws_wire_reject = 'OFAC MATCH'
    ofac_search_bank = ws_beneficiary_bank
    ofacsrch(ofac_request, ofac_response)
    if ofac_match_found == 'Y':
        if ofac_match_score >= 85:
            ws_ofac_clear = 'N'
            ws_wire_reject = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11(debit_originator: callable, create_wire_message: callable, transmit_wire: callable, record_wire: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    debit_originator()
    create_wire_message()
    transmit_wire()
    record_wire()

def debit_originator_C11(ws_wire_amount: Decimal, ws_wire_fee: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, update_account: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_account_balance -= ws_wire_amount
    ws_account_balance -= ws_wire_fee
    update_account()

def create_wire_message_C11(ws_swift_message: WsSwiftMessageC11, ws_wire_ref: str, ws_wire_date: str, ws_wire_currency: str, ws_wire_amount: Decimal, ws_originator_name: str, ws_originator_account: str, ws_beneficiary_name: str, ws_beneficiary_account: str, ws_beneficiary_bank_bic: str, ws_purpose: str) -> None:
    """COBOL lines: 24320-create_wire_message, INITIALIZE ws_swift_message, etc."""
    ws_swift_message.swift_msg_type = 'MT103'
    ws_swift_message.swift_txn_ref = ws_wire_ref
    ws_swift_message.swift_value_date = ws_wire_date
    ws_swift_message.swift_currency = ws_wire_currency
    ws_swift_message.swift_amount = ws_wire_amount
    ws_swift_message.swift_ordering_cust = ws_originator_name
    ws_swift_message.swift_ordering_ACCT = ws_originator_account
    ws_swift_message.swift_benef_cust = ws_beneficiary_name
    ws_swift_message.swift_benef_ACCT = ws_beneficiary_account
    ws_swift_message.swift_benef_bank = ws_beneficiary_bank_bic
    ws_swift_message.swift_remit_info = ws_purpose

def transmit_wire_C11(ws_swift_message: WsSwiftMessageC11, ws_swift_response: str, swift_status: str, ws_wire_status: str, swiftsend: callable, reverse_debit: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL lines: 24330-transmit_wire, CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING ws_swift_message, etc."""
    swiftsend(ws_swift_message, ws_swift_response)
    if swift_status == 'ACK':
        ws_wire_status = 'SENT'
    else:
        ws_wire_status = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit()

def record_wire_C11(ws_wire_record: WsWireRecordC11, ws_wire_ref: str, ws_wire_amount: Decimal, ws_wire_status: str, ws_originator_account: str, ws_beneficiary_account: str, ws_process_date: str) -> None:
    """COBOL lines: 24340-record_wire, INITIALIZE ws_wire_record, etc."""
    ws_wire_record.wire_ref = ws_wire_ref
    ws_wire_record.wire_amount = ws_wire_amount
    ws_wire_record.wire_status = ws_wire_status
    ws_wire_record.wire_from_ACCT = ws_originator_account
    ws_wire_record.wire_to_ACCT = ws_beneficiary_account
    ws_wire_record.wire_date = ws_process_date
    # Assuming WRITE wire_record is handled elsewhere as file IO

def reverse_debit_C11(ws_wire_amount: Decimal, ws_wire_fee: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, update_account: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_account_balance += ws_wire_amount
    ws_account_balance += ws_wire_fee
    update_account()

def send_confirmation_C11(ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_wire_ref: str, ws_notif_subject: str, send_notification: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_notif_type = 'wire_confirm'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Wire transfer {ws_wire_ref} completed'
    send_notification()

def reject_wire_C11(ws_wire_status: str, ws_wire_reject_rec: WsWireRejectRecC11, ws_wire_ref: str, ws_wire_reject: str, ws_process_date: str, ws_notif_type: str, send_notification: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_wire_status = 'REJECTED'
    ws_wire_reject_rec.reject_wire_ref = ws_wire_ref
    ws_wire_reject_rec.reject_reason = ws_wire_reject
    ws_wire_reject_rec.reject_date = ws_process_date
    # Assuming WRITE wire_reject_record is handled elsewhere as file IO
    ws_notif_type = 'wire_rejected'
    send_notification()

def ach_processing_C11(receive_ach_file: callable, validate_ach_entries: callable, process_ach_credits: callable, process_ach_debits: callable, generate_ach_return: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    receive_ach_file()
    validate_ach_entries()
    process_ach_credits()
    process_ach_debits()
    generate_ach_return()

def receive_ach_file_C11(ach_input_file: AchInputFileC11, ws_ach_file_header: WsAchFileHeaderC11, ach_file_id: str, ach_creation_date: str, ach_entry_count: int, ws_current_ach_file: str, ws_ach_file_date: str, ws_expected_entries: int) -> None:
    """COBOL lines: 25100-receive_ach_file, OPEN INPUT ach_input_file, etc."""
    # Assuming OPEN INPUT and READ are handled elsewhere as file IO
    # and ach_input_file provides the data
    ws_ach_file_header.ach_file_id = ach_file_id
    ws_ach_file_header.ach_creation_date = ach_creation_date
    ws_ach_file_header.ach_entry_count = ach_entry_count
    ws_current_ach_file = ach_file_id
    ws_ach_file_date = ach_creation_date
    ws_expected_entries = ach_entry_count

def validate_ach_entries_C11(ach_input_file: AchInputFileC11, ws_eof_flag: str, validate_single_entry: callable, ws_valid_entries: int, ws_invalid_entries: int, ws_ach_entry: WsAchEntryC11) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_valid_entries = 0
    ws_invalid_entries = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming READ is handled elsewhere as file IO
        # and ach_input_file provides the data
        ach_entry = ach_input_file  # Assuming ach_input_file yields one entry at a time
        if ach_entry:
            ws_ach_entry.ach_routing = ach_entry.ach_routing
            ws_ach_entry.ach_account = ach_entry.ach_account
            ws_ach_entry.ach_amount = ach_entry.ach_amount
            ws_ach_entry.ach_trans_code = ach_entry.ach_trans_code
            ws_ach_entry.ach_trace_number = ach_entry.ach_trace_number
            validate_single_entry()
        else:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def validate_single_entry_C11(ws_ach_entry: WsAchEntryC11, ws_ach_entry_valid: str, ws_ach_return_code: str, ws_valid_entries: int, ws_invalid_entries: int) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_ach_entry_valid = 'Y'
    if not ws_ach_entry.ach_routing.isnumeric():
        ws_ach_entry_valid = 'N'
        ws_ach_return_code = 'R03'
    if ws_ach_entry.ach_account == '':  # Assuming SPACES is an empty string
        ws_ach_entry_valid = 'N'
        ws_ach_return_code = 'R04'
    if ws_ach_entry.ach_amount <= 0:
        ws_ach_entry_valid = 'N'
        ws_ach_return_code = 'R06'
    if ws_ach_entry_valid == 'Y':
        ws_valid_entries += 1
    else:
        ws_invalid_entries += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11(ach_input_file: AchInputFileC11, ws_eof_flag: str, apply_credit: callable, ws_ach_entry: WsAchEntryC11) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming READ is handled elsewhere as file IO
        # and ach_input_file provides the data
        ach_entry = ach_input_file  # Assuming ach_input_file yields one entry at a time
        if ach_entry:
            ws_ach_entry.ach_routing = ach_entry.ach_routing
            ws_ach_entry.ach_account = ach_entry.ach_account
            ws_ach_entry.ach_amount = ach_entry.ach_amount
            ws_ach_entry.ach_trans_code = ach_entry.ach_trans_code
            ws_ach_entry.ach_trace_number = ach_entry.ach_trace_number

            match ws_ach_entry.ach_trans_code:
                case '22' | '23' | '32' | '33':
                    apply_credit()
        else:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def apply_credit_C11(ach_account: str, ws_search_key: str, search_account: callable, ws_found_flag: str, ach_amount: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, update_account: callable, ws_credits_posted: int, ws_total_credits: Decimal, ws_ach_return_code: str, create_return_entry: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_search_key = ach_account
    search_account()
    if ws_found_flag == 'Y':
        ws_account_balance += ach_amount
        update_account()
        ws_credits_posted += 1
        ws_total_credits += ach_amount
    else:
        ws_ach_return_code = 'R04'
        create_return_entry()

def process_ach_debits_C11(ach_input_file: AchInputFileC11, ws_eof_flag: str, apply_debit: callable, ws_ach_entry: WsAchEntryC11) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # Assuming READ is handled elsewhere as file IO
        # and ach_input_file provides the data
        ach_entry = ach_input_file  # Assuming ach_input_file yields one entry at a time
        if ach_entry:
            ws_ach_entry.ach_routing = ach_entry.ach_routing
            ws_ach_entry.ach_account = ach_entry.ach_account
            ws_ach_entry.ach_amount = ach_entry.ach_amount
            ws_ach_entry.ach_trans_code = ach_entry.ach_trans_code
            ws_ach_entry.ach_trace_number = ach_entry.ach_trace_number
            match ws_ach_entry.ach_trans_code:
                case '27' | '28' | '37' | '38':
                    apply_debit()
        else:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def apply_debit_C11(ach_account: str, ws_search_key: str, search_account: callable, ws_found_flag: str, ws_account_balance: Decimal, ach_amount: Decimal, update_account: callable, ws_debits_posted: int, ws_total_debits: Decimal, ws_ach_return_code: str, create_return_entry: callable) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_search_key = ach_account
    search_account()
    if ws_found_flag == 'Y':
        if ws_account_balance >= ach_amount:
            ws_account_balance -= ach_amount
            update_account()
            ws_debits_posted += 1
            ws_total_debits += ach_amount
        else:
            ws_ach_return_code = 'R01'
            create_return_entry()
    else:
        ws_ach_return_code = 'R04'
        create_return_entry()

def generate_ach_return_C11(ws_return_count: int, create_return_file: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL lines: 25500-generate_ach_return, IF ws_return_count > 0, etc."""
    if ws_return_count > 0:
        create_return_file()

    """COBOL lines: 25510-create_return_entry, INITIALIZE ws_ach_return_entry, etc."""
    ws_ach_return_entry.return_orig_trace = ach_trace_number
    ws_ach_return_entry.return_code = ws_ach_return_code
    ws_ach_return_entry.return_amount = ach_amount
    ws_ach_return_entry.return_account = ach_account
    ws_return_count += 1
    # Assuming WRITE ach_return_record is handled elsewhere as file IO

    """COBOL lines: 25510-create_return_file, OPEN OUTPUT ach_return_file, etc."""
    # Assuming OPEN OUTPUT and CLOSE are handled elsewhere as file IO
    write_return_header()
    write_return_entries()
    write_return_trailer()

def statement_generation_C11(prepare_statement_data: callable, generate_account_summary: callable, generate_transaction_detail: callable, calculate_statement_totals: callable, format_statement: callable, deliver_statement: callable) -> None:

def prepare_statement_data_C11(ws_stmt_date: str, ws_stmt_start_date: int, ws_stmt_end_date: str, ws_stmt_trans_count: int, ws_stmt_credit_total: Decimal, ws_stmt_debit_total: Decimal) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    ws_stmt_date = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")  # Assuming current_date returns YYYYMMDD
    ws_stmt_start_date = datetime.date(int(ws_stmt_date[:4]), int(ws_stmt_date[4:6]), int(ws_stmt_date[6:8])).toordinal() - 30
    ws_stmt_end_date = ws_stmt_date
    ws_stmt_trans_count = 0
    ws_stmt_credit_total = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_stmt_debit_total = Decimal('0.00')

def logging_utilities_C13() -> None:
    """"""
           99800-logging_utilities"""
    """COBOL logic"""
           99810-log_info
    """COBOL logic"""
           99820-log_warning
    """COBOL logic"""
           99830-log_error
    """COBOL logic"""
           99900-error_handling
    """COBOL logic"""
           99910-format_error
    """
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR, WS_ERROR_CODE, WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_FORMATTED_ERROR = f'ERROR: {WS_ERROR_CODE} - {WS_ERROR_MSG}' # STRING 'ERROR: ' DELIMITED SIZE ..."""
""""""
def display_error_C13() -> None:"""
    """"""
           99920-display_error"""
    """
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR
    print(WS_FORMATTED_ERROR) # DISPLAY ws_formatted_error"""
""""""
def write_error_log_C13() -> None:"""
    """"""
           99930-write_error_log"""
    """COBOL logic"""
           32000-treasury_management
    """COBOL logic"""
           32100-calculate_cash_position
    """"""
    global WS_CASH_POSITION"""
    WS_CASH_POSITION = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_cash_position
    sum_vault_cash_C13() # PERFORM 32110-sum_vault_cash
    sum_fed_account_C13() # PERFORM 32120-sum_fed_account
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13() # PERFORM 32130-sum_correspondent_balances

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """"""
           32110-sum_vault_cash"""
    """COBOL logic"""
           32120-sum_fed_account
    """
    global FED_ACCOUNT_FILE, WS_FED_BALANCE, WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_FED_BALANCE = read_fed_account_file() # READ fed_account_file INTO ws_fed_balance
    WS_CASH_POSITION += WS_FED_BALANCE['balance'] # ADD ws_fed_balance TO ws_cash_position"""
""""""

    WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS = Decimal("0.00") # MOVE ZEROES TO ws_projected_outflows
    project_loan_payments_C13() #

    """COBOL logic"""

    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL = Decimal("0.00")

    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL += None  # TODO: was WS_AOCI
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= None  # TODO: was WS_GOODWILL
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= None  # TODO: was WS_INTANGIBLES
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL -= None  # TODO: was WS_DTA_DEDUCTION

def calculate_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""34200-risk_weighted_assets
    MOVE ZEROES TO ws_risk_weighted_assets
    PERFORM 34210-credit_rwa
    PERFORM 34220-market_rwa
    PERFORM 34230-operational_rwa."""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS = Decimal("0.00")
    credit_rwa_C14()
    market_rwa_C14()
    operational_rwa_C14()

def perform_reconciliation(ws_gl_control_bal: Decimal, ws_subledger_total: Decimal):
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_RECON_DIFF
    WS_RECON_DIFF = ws_gl_control_bal - ws_subledger_total
    if WS_RECON_DIFF != 0:
        log_recon_exception_C15()

WS_IC_COUNT = 0
WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

@dataclass
class WS_IC_BALANCE_C15:
    """Structure of Intercompany Balance"""
    IC_FROM_ENTITY: str = ""
    IC_TO_ENTITY: str = ""
    IC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

WS_IC_ARRAY = [WS_IC_BALANCE_C15() for _ in range(100)] # Assuming max 100 entries. Adjust as needed
WS_IC_BALANCE = WS_IC_BALANCE_C15()

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_ARRAY, WS_IC_BALANCE

    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        record = read_intercompany_file() # Assuming read_intercompany_file returns WS_IC_BALANCE or None if EOF
        if record is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_IC_COUNT += 1
            WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_COUNT - 1] = WS_IC_BALANCE_C15(record.IC_FROM_ENTITY, record.IC_TO_ENTITY, record.IC_AMOUNT)

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_intercompany_file() -> WS_IC_BALANCE_C15 | None:
    """"""
    Dummy function to simulate reading an intercompany file"""
    Replace with actual file reading logic"""
    """"""
    global WS_IC_BALANCE"""
    if WS_IC_COUNT < 5: # Simulate reading 5 records"""
      WS_IC_BALANCE = WS_IC_BALANCE_C15(f"Entity{WS_IC_COUNT+1}A", f"Entity{WS_IC_COUNT+1}B", Decimal(str((WS_IC_COUNT+1)*100)))
      return WS_IC_BALANCE
    else:
      return None  # Simulate end of file

WS_IC_IDX = 0
def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_COUNT
    WS_IC_IDX = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        find_ic_counterpart_C15()
        WS_IC_IDX += 1

WS_IC_IDX2 = 0
WS_SEARCH_FROM = ""
WS_SEARCH_TO = ""
WS_IC_DIFF = 0

def find_ic_counterpart_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_IDX2, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, WS_IC_ARRAY

    WS_SEARCH_FROM = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY
    WS_SEARCH_TO = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY
    WS_IC_IDX2 = 1

    while WS_IC_IDX2 <= WS_IC_COUNT:
        if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_FROM_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_TO:
            if WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_TO_ENTITY == WS_SEARCH_FROM:
                WS_IC_DIFF = WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX - 1].IC_AMOUNT + WS_IC_ARRAY[WS_IC_IDX2 - 1].IC_AMOUNT
                if WS_IC_DIFF != 0:
                    log_ic_diff_C15()
                break  # EXIT PERFORM
        WS_IC_IDX2 += 1

    global WS_NOSTRO_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_NOSTRO_ITEM

    WS_NOSTRO_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        record = read_nostro_statement_file() # Placeholder - replace with actual read
        if record is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_NOSTRO_COUNT += 1

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

@dataclass
class WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15:
    """Structure of an Audit Record"""
    WS_AUDIT_ID: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP: str = ""
    WS_AUDIT_USER: str = ""
    WS_AUDIT_ACTION: str = ""
    WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID: Decimal = Decimal("0")
    WS_AUDIT_TABLE: str = ""
    WS_AUDIT_KEY: str = ""
    WS_AUDIT_OLD_VALUE: str = ""
    WS_AUDIT_NEW_VALUE: str = ""

WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15()
WS_USER_ID = "TestUser" # Placeholder
WS_ACTION_TYPE = "TestAction" # Placeholder
WS_SESSION_ID = 12345 # Placeholder

def log_user_action_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    import random
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD, WS_USER_ID, WS_ACTION_TYPE, WS_SESSION_ID

    WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15()
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_ID = Decimal(str(random.random() * 99999999999))
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d%H%M%S")  # YYYYMMDDHHMMSS
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_USER  = None  # TODO: was WS_USER_ID
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_ACTION  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACTION_TYPE
    WS_AUDIT_RECORD.WS_AUDIT_SESSION_ID = Decimal(str(WS_SESSION_ID))

    write_audit_record(WS_AUDIT_RECORD) # Assuming write_audit_record is defined

def write_audit_record(record: WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15) -> None:
    """Placeholder for writing the audit record to a file or database."""

WS_END_OF_MONTH = 'N'
WS_ARCHIVE_DATE = "20240101"

def archive_audit_logs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_END_OF_MONTH
    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
        move_to_archive_C15()
        compress_archive_C15()

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_AUDIT_RECORD, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        record = read_audit_file()
        if record is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if record.WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                write_archive_audit_record(record)
                delete_audit_file(record) # Assuming delete_audit_file is defined

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_audit_file() -> WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15 | None:
    """Dummy function for reading audit file. Replace with actual implementation."""
    global WS_AUDIT_RECORD
    if WS_NOSTRO_COUNT < 3: # Simulate 3 records
        WS_AUDIT_RECORD = WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15(WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = "20231231000000")
        return WS_AUDIT_RECORD
    else:
        return None

def write_archive_audit_record(record: WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15) -> None:
  """Placeholder for writing archived records"""
  print(f"Writing archived record: {record}")

def delete_audit_file(record: WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15) -> None:
  """Placeholder for deleting audit record"""
  print(f"Deleting audit record: {record}")

    cpu_metrics_C15()
    memory_metrics_C15()
    io_metrics_C15()
    transaction_metrics_C15()

WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = 0
WS_CPU_ALERT = 'N'

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT

    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = getcpu() # Assuming getcpu returns CPU utilization

    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'

  """Dummy function to simulate GETCPU"""
  return 85

WS_TRANS_COUNT = 100 # Placeholder
WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS = 60 # Placeholder
WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME = 500 # Placeholder
WS_TPS = 0
WS_AVG_RESPONSE = 0

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME, WS_TPS, WS_AVG_RESPONSE

    WS_TPS = WS_TRANS_COUNT / WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS
    WS_AVG_RESPONSE = WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME / WS_TRANS_COUNT

WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD = 5 # Placeholder
WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD = 1 # Placeholder
WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'N'
WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'N'

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD, WS_TPS, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD, WS_PERF_DEGRADED, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW

    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'
    if WS_TPS < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y'

def generate_alerts_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CPU_ALERT, WS_MEMORY_ALERT, WS_PERF_DEGRADED

    if WS_CPU_ALERT == 'Y':
        send_cpu_alert_C15()
    if WS_MEMORY_ALERT == 'Y':
        send_memory_alert_C15()
    if WS_PERF_DEGRADED == 'Y':
        send_perf_alert_C15()

WS_NOTIF_TYPE = ""
WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = ""
WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = ""

def send_cpu_alert_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_CPU_UTILIZATION

    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'high_cpu'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f"ALERT: CPU utilization at {WS_CPU_UTILIZATION}%"
    send_notification_C15()  # Assuming send_notification is defined
